>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:5:# The "pre-rebase" hook is run just before "git rebase" starts doing
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:6:# its job, and can prevent the command from running by exiting with
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:7:# non-zero status.
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:8:#
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:9:# The hook is called with the following parameters:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:10:#
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:11:# $1 -- the upstream the series was forked from.
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:12:# $2 -- the branch being rebased (or empty when rebasing the current branch).
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:13:#
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:14:# This sample shows how to prevent topic branches that are already
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:15:# merged to 'next' branch from getting rebased, because allowing it
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:16:# would result in rebasing already published history.
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:17:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:18:publish=next
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:19:basebranch="$1"
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:20:if test "$#" = 2
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:21:then
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:22:	topic="refs/heads/$2"
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:23:else
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:24:	topic=`git symbolic-ref HEAD` ||
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:25:	exit 0 ;# we do not interrupt rebasing detached HEAD
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:26:fi
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:27:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:28:case "$topic" in
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:29:refs/heads/??/*)
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:30:	;;
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:31:*)
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:32:	exit 0 ;# we do not interrupt others.
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:33:	;;

>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:35:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:36:# Now we are dealing with a topic branch being rebased

>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:38:

>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:51:fi
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:52:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:53:# Is topic ever merged to next?  If so you should not be rebasing it.
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:54:only_next_1=`git rev-list ^master "^$topic" ${publish} | sort`
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:55:only_next_2=`git rev-list ^master           ${publish} | sort`
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:56:if test "$only_next_1" = "$only_next_2"

>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:59:	if test -z "$not_in_topic"
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:60:	then
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:61:		echo >&2 "$topic is already up to date with master"
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:62:		exit 1 ;# we could allow it, but there is no point.
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:63:	else
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:64:		exit 0
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:65:	fi
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:66:else
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:67:	not_in_next=`git rev-list --pretty=oneline ^${publish} "$topic"`
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:68:	/usr/bin/perl -e '
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:69:		my $topic = $ARGV[0];
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:70:		my $msg = "* $topic has commits already merged to public branch:\n";
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:71:		my (%not_in_next) = map {
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:72:			/^([0-9a-f]+) /;
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:73:			($1 => 1);

>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:80:				if ($msg) {
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:81:					print STDERR $msg;

>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:85:			}
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:86:		}
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:87:	' "$topic" "$not_in_next" "$not_in_master"
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:88:	exit 1
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:89:fi
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:90:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:91:<<\DOC_END
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:92:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:93:This sample hook safeguards topic branches that have been
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:94:published from being rewound.
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:95:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:96:The workflow assumed here is:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:97:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:98: * Once a topic branch forks from "master", "master" is never
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:99:   merged into it again (either directly or indirectly).
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:100:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:101: * Once a topic branch is fully cooked and merged into "master",
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:102:   it is deleted.  If you need to build on top of it to correct
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:103:   earlier mistakes, a new topic branch is created by forking at
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:104:   the tip of the "master".  This is not strictly necessary, but
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:105:   it makes it easier to keep your history simple.
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:106:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:107: * Whenever you need to test or publish your changes to topic
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:108:   branches, merge them into "next" branch.
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:109:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:110:The script, being an example, hardcodes the publish branch name
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:111:to be "next", but it is trivial to make it configurable via
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:112:$GIT_DIR/config mechanism.
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:113:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:114:With this workflow, you would want to know:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:115:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:116:(1) ... if a topic branch has ever been merged to "next".  Young
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:117:    topic branches can have stupid mistakes you would rather
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:118:    clean up before publishing, and things that have not been
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:119:    merged into other branches can be easily rebased without
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:120:    affecting other people.  But once it is published, you would
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:121:    not want to rewind it.
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:122:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:123:(2) ... if a topic branch has been fully merged to "master".
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:124:    Then you can delete it.  More importantly, you should not
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:125:    build on top of it -- other people may already want to
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:126:    change things related to the topic as patches against your
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:127:    "master", so if you need further changes, it is better to
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:128:    fork the topic (perhaps with the same name) afresh from the
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:129:    tip of "master".
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:130:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:131:Let's look at this example:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:132:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:133:		   o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o "next"
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:134:		  /       /           /           /
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:135:		 /   a---a---b A     /           /
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:136:		/   /               /           /
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:137:	       /   /   c---c---c---c B         /
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:138:	      /   /   /             \         /
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:139:	     /   /   /   b---b C     \       /
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:140:	    /   /   /   /             \     /
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:141:    ---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o "master"
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:142:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:143:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:144:A, B and C are topic branches.
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:145:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:146: * A has one fix since it was merged up to "next".
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:147:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:148: * B has finished.  It has been fully merged up to "master" and "next",
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:149:   and is ready to be deleted.
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:150:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:151: * C has not merged to "next" at all.
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:152:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:153:We would want to allow C to be rebased, refuse A, and encourage
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:154:B to be deleted.
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:155:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:156:To compute (1):
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:157:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:158:	git rev-list ^master ^topic next
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:159:	git rev-list ^master        next
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:160:
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/.theme/.git/hooks/pre-rebase.sample:161:	if these match, topic has not merged in next at all.

>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/macro_PauseResumeCancelM600.cfg:63:##    Will not allow you to accidentally execute pause or resume twice.
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/macro_PauseResumeCancelM600.cfg:64:##    Allows you to take other actions during the pause without messing anything up.
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/macro_PauseResumeCancelM600.cfg:65:##        You can move toolhead around during the pause, run load/unload filament macros etc. It will automatically return to its original position before resuming.
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/macro_PauseResumeCancelM600.cfg:66:##        It also automatically restores your gcode state (absolute vs relative extrusion mode, etc), should it be changed during the pause by another macro.
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/macro_PauseResumeCancelM600.cfg:67:##    Primes the nozzle while traveling back to resume the print, wiping the excess along the way. This just results in one little string to pick off.
>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/macro_PauseResumeCancelM600.cfg:68:##    Sets the idle timeout to 12 hours during the pause, and returns it to your configured value upon resume.

>> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/macro_PauseResumeCancelM600.cfg:73:##        * *I highly advise keeping this functionality, even though it can be a bit annoying at times. It's a safety feature. >> /home/ajs/printer_data/config/macro_PauseResumeCancelM600.cfg.bak:63:##    Will not allow you to accidentally execute pause or resume twice.
